#  METADATA Data Description:  
#
#  Filename: appendix6d.txt
#
#  This file contains the data set for organochlorine pesticides in whole fish
#  from streams sampled by the NAWQA Program, 1992-2001, and used as the basis 
#  of the occurrence and distribution assessment, as summarized in USGS Circular 1291, 
#  Pesticides in the Nation's Streams and Ground Water, 1992-2001: The  Quality of Our 
#  Nation's Waters, at http://ca.water.usgs.gov/pnsp/pubs/circ1291/. The data set 
#  contains one sample per site. All samples are composites of 5–8 individual whole
#  fish of the same species (taxon). 
#
# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#

#           Note: The row in the data file that follows the row of variable names describes the 
#                 width of the field and the data type. "s" indicates a text variable, 
#                 "d" indicates a date variable, and "n" indicates a numeric variable.
#                 For example: 15s indicates a 0- to 15-character text variable whereas
#                 9n indicates a 0- to 9-digit numeric variable.
#

#
#       Attribute label: STAID
#
#       Attribute description: Station identifier
#       Source: NAWQA Data Warehouse water-quality data retrieval 7-30-2002
#
#
#       Attribute label: PSTAID
#
#       Attribute description: Primary station identifier
#       Source: NAWQA Data Warehouse water-quality data retrieval 7-30-2002
#
#            Note: STAID should be used for whole-fish data analysis. STAID
#                  is the actual location of the sample. For 665 of 700 sites, 
#                  PSTAID is equal to STAID. STAID not equal to PSTAID
#                  occurs where either (1) the location of sample collection
#                  changed during the 1992–2001 study period, or (2) the sample
#                  could not be collected at PSTAID, so it was collected at
#                  a nearby location. In either case, STAID is considered
#                  equivalent to PSTAID.
#
#
#       Attribute label: SU_YEAR
#
#       Attribute description: Year during which this study unit began its intensive 
#            sampling as part of NAWQA rotational investigations, YYYY
#       Source: NAWQA Data Warehouse water-quality data retrieval 7-30-2002
#
#
#       Attribute label: SNAME
#
#       Attribute description: Name of data collection station (STAID)
#       Source: NAWQA Data Warehouse site information retrieval 8-18-2005
#
#
#       Attribute label: LANDUSE
#
#       Attribute description: Drainage basin land use class
#       Source: NAWQA national classification system
#
#                Code   Description
#                -----  -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#                Agricultural     Greater than 50% agricultural land and less than or equal to 5% urban land
#                Undeveloped   Less than or equal to 25% agricultural land and less than or equal to 5% urban land
#                Urban            Greater than 25% urban land and less than or equal to 25% agricultural land
#                Mixed            All other combinations of land use
#
#
#       Attribute label: DATETIME
#
#       Attribute description: Date and time of fish sample collection 
#            at this site, YYYY.MM.DD TTTT
#       Source: NAWQA Data Warehouse water-quality data retrieval 7-30-2002
#
#
#       Attribute label: MEDIM
#
#       Attribute description: Medium of sample
#       Source: NAWQA Data Warehouse water-quality data retrieval 7-30-2002
#
#                Code   Description
#                -----  -------------------------
#                C       Animal tissue
#                X      Quality control sample (replicate)-- Animal tissue
#
#
#       Attribute label: TAXON
#
#       Attribute description: Common name of fish species (taxon) sampled. 
#            If common name was not available, the scientific name was used. 
#            Taxa from the National Target Taxa List were targeted for collection
#            at NAWQA sites. 
#       Source: NAWQA Data Warehouse water-quality data retrieval 7-30-2002
#
#
#       Attribute label: Rnnnnn (All variables of the form Rnnnnn, where nnnnn is the 5-digit parameter
#                         code used to identify variables in the USGS National Water Information System)
#
#       Attribute description: Remark code associated with each parameter value
#       Source: NAWQA Data Warehouse water-quality data retrieval 7-30-2002
#
#                Code     Description
#                -----    ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#                <        Not detected at or above the concentration in Pnnnnn cell.
#                >        Detected at a concentration above that in Pnnnnn cell.
#                -         Detected at concentration equal to that in Pnnnnn cell.
#                .         Missing data.
#                E        Detected. Concentration is estimated. Estimated concentration may result from
#                          any of the following reasons:
#                          1. The compound is characterized as a "poor performer" in the method on the 
#                             basis of laboratory QC samples. In general compounds with less than 60 %
#                             recovery, greater than 120 % recovery, or greater than 25 % relative standard
#                             deviation of recovery are considered poor performers. All detections of
#                             these compounds are remarked E.
#                          2. The compound was detected at a concentration less than the reporting level
#                             or less than the lowest calibration standard.
#                          3. The sample was diluted to bring the concentration into the calibration range.
#
#
#       Attribute label: Pnnnnn (All variables of the form Pnnnnn, where nnnnn is the 5-digit parameter
#                         code used to identify variables in the USGS National Water Information System)
#
#       Attribute description: Value of chemical analysis
#       Source: NAWQA Data Warehouse water-quality data retrieval 7-30-2002
#
#       Attribute subdescription: Parameter codes, names, and units of measurement
#                                   
#               Parameter Code   Name                        Units
#               ------------------------   -----------------------------   ---------------------
#               P49289                Lipids                 Percent wet weight
#               P49353                Aldrin                 microgram per kilogram wet weight
#               P49355                Toxaphene           microgram per kilogram wet weight
#               P49356                Pentachloroanisole      microgram per kilogram wet weight
#               P49357                Oxychlordane        microgram per kilogram wet weight
#               P49358                trans-Nonachlor        microgram per kilogram wet weight
#               P49359                cis-Nonachlor        microgram per kilogram wet weight
#               P49360                Mirex                  microgram per kilogram wet weight
#               P49361                p,p´-Methoxychlor        microgram per kilogram wet weight
#               P49362                o,p´-Methoxychlor        microgram per kilogram wet weight
#               P49363                Lindane (gamma-HCH)     microgram per kilogram wet weight
#               P49364                delta-HCH            microgram per kilogram wet weight
#               P49365                beta-HCH            microgram per kilogram wet weight
#               P49366                alpha-HCH           microgram per kilogram wet weight
#               P49367                Hexachlorobenzene     microgram per kilogram wet weight
#               P49368                Heptachlor epoxide     microgram per kilogram wet weight
